## CX — Closeness between party and average Congress ideo / (this is to determine which party would win a contingent election in Congress if no candidate wins the Electoral College!) / Federalist

**Rows 91–157**

```
=CX90+4
```

),
-0.5,
0

OR(GV4=MAX(GV4:HB4),GV4=MAX(GV4:HB4),GV4=MAX(GV4:HB4))
),
AND(
AO5>1900,
OR(GV4>0.13,GV4>0.13)
)
),
MAX(0,MIN(1-GJ5-GK5,1-IF(GJ4>0.98,RANDBETWEEN(70,90)/100,RANDBETWEEN(70,90)/100))),
0
),
IF(GN4>0.8,
RANDBETWEEN(35,55)/100,
MIN(1,MAX(0,IF(AE5="Socialist",GN4+0.5*(AN5-0.5),GN4-0.5*(AN5-0.5))))
)
)
+IF(AS4="Socialist",GQ4/2,0)
+IF(AND(AK5<-0.3,AS4="Socialist"),RAND()*(0.14)-100,0)
))
```

**Rows 92–158**

```
=IF(MG91>0,MIN(0.95,GN91+((IF(GN$88="YES",1,0)+1.8)*RAND()/100)),0)*(IF(RAND()<1/30,0.98,1))
```

## HY — J. Republican / JRepIdeo

```
=MIN(10,MAX(-10,
(
(6*HY5)+

IF(GV5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),-9,
IF(GW5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),-6.5,
IF(GX5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),-3.5,
IF(GY5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),0,
IF(GZ5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),3.5,
IF(HA5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),6.5,
IF(HB5=MAX($GV5:$HB5),9,0)))))))

+

IF(IA5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),-9,
IF(IB5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),-6.5,
IF(IC5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),-3.5,
IF(ID5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),0,
IF(IE5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),3.5,
IF(IF5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),6.5,
IF(IG5=MAX($IA5:$IG5),9,0)))))))

)/8

+(RAND()*2-1)*IF(AK5<0,$HW$9*ABS(AK5),0.8)*IF(AE5=$HY$1, 0.5, 1.5)
+if(AE5=$HY$1, 0, ($HW$13*-AG5))

))
```

=IF(AND(TZ4="Empty",TY5="Empty"),"Empty",

    IF(RAND()<0.1,RAND()-0.5+($TV5+$TV5+$TW5)/3,"Empty"),
    IF(RAND()<0.2,RAND()-0.5+($TV5+$TV5+$TW5)/3,
        TZ4+RAND()-0.5
    )
))
```
